# Linux — inferred

The **5,721** projects that probably run on Linux but do not say so. Each verdict here comes from the install route or the language runtime, never from a release asset or from the README — that is what puts them on this page rather than with the [1,985 confirmed ones](linux.md).

Read `Why` before trusting a row. A portable runtime is a good bet and not a promise.

[← Linux](linux.md) · [← back to the mega list](../README.md)

Page **5** of 8, because this list is longer than the 512 KB GitHub will render in one file. In order: [1](linux-inferred.md) · [2](linux-inferred-2.md) · [3](linux-inferred-3.md) · [4](linux-inferred-4.md) · **5** · [6](linux-inferred-6.md) · [7](linux-inferred-7.md) · [8](linux-inferred-8.md) — [continue on page 6 →](linux-inferred-6.md)
